===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 83-105
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
176 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
177 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
178 |                                    String sourceString,
179 |                                    Config config,
180 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
181 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
182 |         config, errorReporter);
183 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
184 | 
185 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
186 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
187 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
188 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
189 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
190 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
191 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
197 | 
198 |     return irNode;
199 |   }

87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
88 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }

Documentation:

Method code:
345 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
346 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
347 |     // two cases:
348 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
349 |     // and
350 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
351 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
352 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
353 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
354 |     //
355 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
356 |     //
357 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
358 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
359 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
360 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
361 |     //
362 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
363 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
364 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
365 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
366 | 
367 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
368 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
369 |     }
370 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
371 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 349-379
Documentation:
/**
   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources
   * are in modules.
   */
Method code:
349 |   public <T extends SourceFile> void initModules(
350 |       List<T> externs, List<JSModule> modules, CompilerOptions options) {
351 |     initOptions(options);
352 | 
353 |     checkFirstModule(modules);
354 |     fillEmptyModules(modules);
355 | 
356 |     this.externs = makeCompilerInput(externs, true);
357 | 
358 |     // Generate the module graph, and report any errors in the module
359 |     // specification as errors.
360 |     this.modules = modules;
361 |     if (modules.size() > 1) {
362 |       try {
363 |         this.moduleGraph = new JSModuleGraph(modules);
364 |       } catch (JSModuleGraph.ModuleDependenceException e) {
365 |         // problems with the module format.  Report as an error.  The
366 |         // message gives all details.
367 |         report(JSError.make(MODULE_DEPENDENCY_ERROR,
368 |                 e.getModule().getName(), e.getDependentModule().getName()));
369 |         return;
370 |       }
371 |     } else {
372 |       this.moduleGraph = null;
373 |     }
374 | 
375 |     this.inputs = getAllInputsFromModules(modules);
376 |     initBasedOnOptions();
377 | 
378 |     initInputsByIdMap();
379 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldAssign(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 458-532
Documentation:

Method code:
458 |   private Node tryFoldAssign(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
459 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isAssign());
460 | 
461 |     if (!late) {
462 |       return n;
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     // Tries to convert x = x + y -> x += y;
466 |     if (!right.hasChildren() ||
467 |         right.getFirstChild().getNext() != right.getLastChild()) {
468 |       // RHS must have two children.
469 |       return n;
470 |     }
471 | 
472 |     if (mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
473 |       return n;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     Node newRight;
477 |     if (areNodesEqualForInlining(left, right.getFirstChild())) {
478 |       newRight = right.getLastChild();
479 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isCommutative(right.getType()) &&
480 |           areNodesEqualForInlining(left, right.getLastChild())) {
481 |       newRight = right.getFirstChild();
482 |     } else {
483 |       return n;
484 |     }
485 | 
486 |     int newType = -1;
487 |     switch (right.getType()) {
488 |       case Token.ADD:
489 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_ADD;
490 |         break;
491 |       case Token.BITAND:
492 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITAND;
493 |         break;
494 |       case Token.BITOR:
495 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITOR;
496 |         break;
497 |       case Token.BITXOR:
498 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR;
499 |         break;
500 |       case Token.DIV:
501 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_DIV;
502 |         break;
503 |       case Token.LSH:
504 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_LSH;
505 |         break;
506 |       case Token.MOD:
507 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MOD;
508 |         break;
509 |       case Token.MUL:
510 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MUL;
511 |         break;
512 |       case Token.RSH:
513 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_RSH;
514 |         break;
515 |       case Token.SUB:
516 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_SUB;
517 |         break;
518 |       case Token.URSH:
519 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_URSH;
520 |         break;
521 |       default:
522 |         return n;
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     Node newNode = new Node(newType,
526 |         left.detachFromParent(), newRight.detachFromParent());
527 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNode);
528 | 
529 |     reportCodeChange();
530 | 
531 |     return newNode;
532 |   }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0115 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.shouldRetraverse(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 137-151
Documentation:

Method code:
137 |   private boolean shouldRetraverse(Node node) {
138 |     if (node.getParent() != null && node.isFunction() || node.isScript()) {
139 |       ScopeState state = traversalState.peek();
140 |       if (state.changed) {
141 |         // prepare to re-visit the scope:
142 |         // when revisiting, only visit the immediate scope
143 |         // this reduces the cost of getting to a fixed
144 |         // point in global scope.
145 |         state.changed = false;
146 |         state.traverseChildScopes = false;
147 |         return true;
148 |       }
149 |     }
150 |     return false;
151 |   }

Method code:
154 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
155 |     switch (n.getType()) {
156 |       // Childless expressions
157 |       case Token.FALSE:

159 |       case Token.THIS:
160 |       case Token.TRUE:
161 |         validateChildless(n);

163 |

167 |       case Token.NEG:
168 |       case Token.NOT:
169 |       case Token.INC:
170 |       case Token.DEC:
171 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
172 |       case Token.VOID:
173 |       case Token.BITNOT:
174 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
175 |         return;
176 | 
177 |       // General binary ops
178 |       case Token.COMMA:
179 |       case Token.OR:
180 |       case Token.AND:
181 |       case Token.BITOR:
182 |       case Token.BITXOR:
183 |       case Token.BITAND:
184 |       case Token.EQ:
185 |       case Token.NE:
186 |       case Token.SHEQ:
187 |       case Token.SHNE:
188 |       case Token.LT:
189 |       case Token.GT:
190 |       case Token.LE:
191 |       case Token.GE:
192 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
193 |       case Token.IN:
194 |       case Token.LSH:
195 |       case Token.RSH:
196 |       case Token.URSH:
197 |       case Token.SUB:
198 |       case Token.ADD:
199 |       case Token.MUL:
200 |       case Token.MOD:
201 |       case Token.DIV:
202 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
203 |         return;
204 | 
205 |       // Assignments
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
218 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
219 |         return;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.HOOK:
222 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
223 |         return;
224 | 
225 |       // Node types that require special handling
226 |       case Token.STRING:
227 |         validateString(n);
228 |         return;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.NUMBER:
231 |         validateNumber(n);
232 |         return;
233 | 
234 |       case Token.NAME:
235 |         validateName(n);
236 |         return;
237 | 
238 |       case Token.GETELEM:
239 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
240 |         return;
241 | 
242 |       case Token.GETPROP:
243 |         validateGetProp(n);
244 |         return;
245 | 
246 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
247 |         validateArrayLit(n);
248 |         return;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
251 |         validateObjectLit(n);
252 |         return;
253 | 
254 |       case Token.REGEXP:
255 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
256 |         return;
257 | 
258 |       case Token.CALL:
259 |         validateCall(n);
260 |         return;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NEW:
263 |         validateNew(n);
264 |         return;
265 | 
266 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
267 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
268 |         return;
269 | 
270 |       default:
271 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
272 |             + Token.name(n.getType()), n);
273 |     }
274 |   }

773 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
774 |     boolean valid = false;
775 |     if (i == 0) {
776 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
777 |     } else if (i == 1) {
778 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();

781 |     }
782 |     if (!valid) {

===== M0128 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1240-1363
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1240 |   Node parseInputs() {
1241 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1242 | 
1243 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1244 |     // individual file parse trees.
1245 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1246 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1247 |     }
1248 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1249 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1250 |     }
1251 | 
1252 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1253 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1254 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1255 | 
1256 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1257 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1258 | 
1259 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1260 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1261 | 
1262 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1263 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1264 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1265 |     }
1266 | 
1267 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1268 | 
1269 |     try {
1270 |       // Parse externs sources.
1271 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1272 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1273 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1274 |           return null;
1275 |         }
1276 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1277 |       }
1278 | 
1279 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1280 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1281 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1285 | 
1286 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1287 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1288 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1289 |           options.closurePass) {
1290 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1291 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1292 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1293 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1294 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1295 |           }
1296 |         }
1297 | 
1298 |         try {
1299 |           inputs =
1300 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1301 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1302 |           staleInputs = true;
1303 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1304 |           report(JSError.make(
1305 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1306 | 
1307 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1308 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1309 |             return null;
1310 |           }
1311 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1312 |           report(JSError.make(
1313 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1314 | 
1315 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1316 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1317 |             return null;
1318 |           }
1319 |         }
1320 |       }
1321 | 
1322 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1323 | 
1324 |       if (staleInputs) {
1325 |         repartitionInputs();
1326 |       }
1327 | 
1328 |       // Build the AST.
1329 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1330 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1331 |         if (n == null) {
1332 |           continue;
1333 |         }
1334 | 
1335 |         if (devMode) {
1336 |           runSanityCheck();
1337 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1338 |             return null;
1339 |           }
1340 |         }
1341 | 
1342 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1343 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1344 | 
1345 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1346 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1347 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1348 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1349 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1350 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1351 |         }
1352 | 
1353 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1354 |       }
1355 | 
1356 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1357 |         return null;
1358 |       }
1359 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1360 |     } finally {
1361 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1362 |     }
1363 |   }

===== M0146 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.visit(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 170-191
Documentation:

Method code:
170 |   public void visit(Node n) {
171 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
172 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
173 | 
174 |     do {
175 |       somethingChanged = false;
176 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
177 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
178 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
179 | 
180 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
181 |           somethingChanged = true;
182 | 
183 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
184 |         }
185 | 
186 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
187 |           return;
188 |         }
189 |       }
190 |     } while(somethingChanged);
191 |   }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1368-1405
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1368 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1369 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1370 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1371 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1372 |           options.closurePass) {
1373 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1374 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1375 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1376 |           continue;
1377 |         }
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1381 | 
1382 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1383 |       if (n == null) {
1384 |         continue;
1385 |       }
1386 | 
1387 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1388 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1389 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1390 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1391 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1392 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1393 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1394 | 
1395 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1396 | 
1397 |         externs.add(input);
1398 |         staleInputs = true;
1399 |       }
1400 |     }
1401 | 
1402 |     if (staleInputs) {
1403 |       repartitionInputs();
1404 |     }
1405 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
484 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
485 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
486 |          current != null;
487 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
488 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
489 |         return true;
490 |       }
491 |     }
492 |     return false;
493 |   }

===== M0212 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 948-1007
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   private void toString(
949 |       StringBuilder sb,
950 |       boolean printSource,
951 |       boolean printAnnotations,
952 |       boolean printType) {
953 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
954 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getString());
957 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
960 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
961 |       // toStringTree.
962 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
963 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
964 |       } else {
965 |         sb.append(first.getString());
966 |       }
967 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |       sb.append(' ');
969 |       sb.append(getDouble());
970 |     }
971 |     if (printSource) {
972 |       int lineno = getLineno();
973 |       if (lineno != -1) {
974 |         sb.append(' ');
975 |         sb.append(lineno);
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printAnnotations) {
980 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |         int type = keys[i];
983 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |         sb.append(" [");
985 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |         sb.append(": ");
987 |         String value;
988 |         switch (type) {
989 |           default:
990 |             value = x.toString();
991 |             break;
992 |         }
993 |         sb.append(value);
994 |         sb.append(']');
995 |       }
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     if (printType) {
999 |       if (jsType != null) {
1000 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |           sb.append(" : ");
1003 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |         }
1005 |       }
1006 |     }
1007 |   }

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 390-425
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
390 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
391 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
392 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
393 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
394 | 
395 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
396 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
397 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
398 | 
399 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
400 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
401 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
402 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
403 |     }
404 | 
405 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
406 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
407 |       if (superClass != null) {
408 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
409 |       }
410 | 
411 |       if (isInterface()) {
412 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
413 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
414 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
415 |           }
416 |         }
417 |       }
418 |     }
419 | 
420 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
421 |       clearCachedValues();
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     return true;
425 |   }

===== M0234 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1042-1070
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1042 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1043 |     // unknown
1044 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1045 |       return true;
1046 |     }
1047 |     // equality
1048 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1049 |       return true;
1050 |     }
1051 |     // all type
1052 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1053 |       return true;
1054 |     }
1055 |     // unions
1056 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1057 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1058 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1059 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1060 |           return true;
1061 |         }
1062 |       }
1063 |     }
1064 |     // proxy types
1065 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1066 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1067 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1068 |     }
1069 |     return false;
1070 |   }

50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1017-1019
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1017 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1018 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1019 |   }

===== M0273 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 614-626
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
614 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
615 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
616 |     if (typeSet == null) {
617 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
618 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
622 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
623 | 
624 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
625 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
626 |   }

===== M0277 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 246-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
246 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
247 |     this.options = options;
248 |     if (errorManager == null) {
249 |       if (outStream == null) {
250 |         setErrorManager(
251 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
252 |       } else {
253 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
254 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
255 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
256 |         setErrorManager(printer);
257 |       }
258 |     }
259 | 
260 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
261 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
262 |       options.checkTypes = true;
263 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
264 |       options.checkTypes = false;
265 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
266 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
267 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
268 |       // parser type warnings.
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
271 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
272 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
276 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
277 |       options.setWarningLevel(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
279 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
283 |       options.setWarningLevel(
284 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
285 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
289 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
290 |     guards.add(
291 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
292 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
293 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
294 | 
295 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
296 | 
297 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
298 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
299 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
300 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
301 |     // error off.
302 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
303 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
304 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
309 |   }

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
878 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
879 |     int count = 0;
880 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
881 |       count++;
882 |     }
883 |

889 |

892 |   }

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1222-1233
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1222 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1223 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1224 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1225 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1226 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1227 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1228 |       } else {
1229 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1230 |       }
1231 |     }
1232 |     return builder.build();
1233 |   }

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 120-285
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.isAssignmentTarget(Node)

Method code:
1409 |   private boolean isAssignmentTarget(Node n) {
1410 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1411 |     if ((NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
1412 |         || parent.isInc()
1413 |         || parent.isDec()) {
1414 |       // If GETPROP/GETELEM is used as assignment target the object literal is
1415 |       // acting as a temporary we can't fold it here:

Original range: 312-334

/**
   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
312 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
313 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
314 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {
315 |       String refName = getReferenceName();
316 |       if (refName == null) {
317 |         // Someone is trying to access the prototype of a structural function.
318 |         // We don't want to give real properties to this prototype, because
319 |         // then it would propagate to all structural functions.
320 |         setPrototypeNoCheck(

322 |            null);

325 |             new PrototypeObjectType(
326 |                 registry,
327 |                 this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype",
328 |                 registry.getNativeObjectType(OBJECT_TYPE),
329 |                 isNativeObjectType()),
330 |             null);
331 |       }
332 |     }
333 |     return (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
334 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 458-474
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
458 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
459 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
460 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
461 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
462 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
463 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
464 |         String inputName = input.getName();
465 | 
466 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
467 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
468 |         // will get an error down the line.
469 |         inputs.add(input);
470 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
471 |       }
472 |     }
473 |     return inputs;
474 |   }

===== M0378 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.traverse(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 113-135
Documentation:

Method code:
113 |   private void traverse(Node node) {
114 |     // The goal here is to avoid retraversing
115 |     // the entire AST to catch newly created opportunities.
116 |     // So we track whether a "unit of code" has changed,
117 |     // and revisit immediately.
118 |     if (!shouldVisit(node)) {
119 |       return;
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     int visits = 0;
123 |     do {
124 |       for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
125 |         traverse(c);
126 |       }
127 | 
128 |       visit(node);
129 |       visits++;
130 | 
131 |       Preconditions.checkState(visits < 10000, "too many interations");
132 |     } while (shouldRetraverse(node));
133 | 
134 |     exitNode(node);
135 |   }

===== M0392 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldArrayAccess(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 1422-1472
Documentation:

Method code:
1422 |   private Node tryFoldArrayAccess(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
1423 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1424 |     // If GETPROP/GETELEM is used as assignment target the array literal is
1425 |     // acting as a temporary we can't fold it here:
1426 |     //    "[][0] += 1"
1427 |     if (isAssignmentTarget(n)) {
1428 |       return n;
1429 |     }
1430 | 
1431 |     if (!right.isNumber()) {
1432 |       // Sometimes people like to use complex expressions to index into
1433 |       // arrays, or strings to index into array methods.
1434 |       return n;
1435 |     }
1436 | 
1437 |     double index = right.getDouble();
1438 |     int intIndex = (int) index;
1439 |     if (intIndex != index) {
1440 |       error(INVALID_GETELEM_INDEX_ERROR, right);
1441 |       return n;
1442 |     }
1443 | 
1444 |     if (intIndex < 0) {
1445 |       error(INDEX_OUT_OF_BOUNDS_ERROR, right);
1446 |       return n;
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     Node current = left.getFirstChild();
1450 |     Node elem = null;
1451 |     for (int i = 0; current != null && i < intIndex; i++) {
1452 |         elem = current;
1453 | 
1454 |       current = current.getNext();
1455 |     }
1456 | 
1457 |     if (elem == null) {
1458 |       error(INDEX_OUT_OF_BOUNDS_ERROR, right);
1459 |       return n;
1460 |     }
1461 | 
1462 |     if (elem.isEmpty()) {
1463 |       elem = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(elem);
1464 |     } else {
1465 |       left.removeChild(elem);
1466 |     }
1467 | 
1468 |     // Replace the entire GETELEM with the value
1469 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, elem);
1470 |     reportCodeChange();
1471 |     return elem;
1472 |   }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0418 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

234 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
235 |       Node propertyNode) {
236 |     if (hasOwnDeclaredProperty(name)) {
237 |       return false;
238 |     }
239 |     Property newProp = new Property(
240 |         name, type, inferred, propertyNode);
241 |     Property oldProp = properties.get(name);
242 |     if (oldProp != null) {
243 |       // This is to keep previously inferred JsDoc info, e.g., in a
244 |       // replaceScript scenario.
245 |       newProp.setJSDocInfo(oldProp.getJSDocInfo());
246 |     }
247 |     properties.put(name, newProp);
248 |     return true;
249 |   }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 99-179
Documentation:

Method code:
99 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
100 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
101 | 
102 |     if (left == null) {
103 |       return subtree;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     Node right = left.getNext();
107 | 
108 |     if (right == null) {
109 |       return subtree;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
113 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.GETPROP:
115 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
116 | 
117 |       case Token.GETELEM:
118 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
119 | 
120 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
121 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
122 | 
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.OR:
125 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
126 | 
127 |       case Token.LSH:
128 |       case Token.RSH:
129 |       case Token.URSH:
130 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
133 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
134 | 
135 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
136 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
137 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
138 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
139 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
140 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
141 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
142 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
143 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
144 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
145 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
146 |         return tryUnfoldAssignOp(subtree, left, right);
147 | 
148 |       case Token.ADD:
149 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
150 | 
151 |       case Token.SUB:
152 |       case Token.DIV:
153 |       case Token.MOD:
154 |         return tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
155 | 
156 |       case Token.MUL:
157 |       case Token.BITAND:
158 |       case Token.BITOR:
159 |       case Token.BITXOR:
160 |         Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
161 |         if (result != subtree) {
162 |           return result;
163 |         }
164 |         return tryFoldLeftChildOp(subtree, left, right);
165 | 
166 |       case Token.LT:
167 |       case Token.GT:
168 |       case Token.LE:
169 |       case Token.GE:
170 |       case Token.EQ:
171 |       case Token.NE:
172 |       case Token.SHEQ:
173 |       case Token.SHNE:
174 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
175 | 
176 |       default:
177 |         return subtree;
178 |     }
179 |   }

192 |   private void tryReduceOperandsForOp(Node n) {
193 |     switch (n.getType()) {
194 |       case Token.ADD:
195 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
196 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
197 |         if (!NodeUtil.mayBeString(left) && !NodeUtil.mayBeString(right)) {
198 |           tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
199 |         }
200 |         break;
201 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
202 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
203 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
204 |         // TODO(johnlenz): convert these to integers.
205 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
212 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
213 |         break;
214 |       case Token.BITNOT:
215 |       case Token.BITOR:
216 |       case Token.BITXOR:
217 |       case Token.BITAND:
218 |       case Token.LSH:
219 |       case Token.RSH:
220 |       case Token.URSH:
221 |       case Token.SUB:
222 |       case Token.MUL:
223 |       case Token.MOD:
224 |       case Token.DIV:
225 |       case Token.POS:
226 |       case Token.NEG:
227 |         tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
228 |         break;
229 |     }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node, specifically excluding its children.
   */
Method code:
1689 |   public Node cloneNode() {
1690 |     Node result;
1691 |     try {
1692 |       result = (Node) super.clone();
1693 |       // PropListItem lists are immutable and can be shared so there is no
1694 |       // need to clone them here.
1695 |       result.next = null;
1696 |       result.first = null;
1697 |       result.last = null;
1698 |       result.parent = null;
1699 |     } catch (CloneNotSupportedException e) {
1700 |       throw new RuntimeException(e.getMessage());
1701 |     }
1702 |     return result;
1703 |   }

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistNoCompileFiles()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1410-1430
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompiler annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1410 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1411 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1412 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1413 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1414 | 
1415 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1416 |       if (n == null) {
1417 |         continue;
1418 |       }
1419 | 
1420 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1421 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1422 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1423 |         staleInputs = true;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 | 
1427 |     if (staleInputs) {
1428 |       repartitionInputs();
1429 |     }
1430 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

376 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
377 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
378 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
379 |       return position;
380 |     } else {

382 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
383 |     }
384 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0500 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }
